part_of_speech: noun
meaning_vi: di sản
definition_en: Traditions, buildings, or objects inherited from earlier generations and valued today.
ipa:
level: C1
topics: culture
collections: foundation,ielts,oxford-5000
collocation: cultural heritage
example_en: The museum helps protect the cultural heritage of the region.
example_vi: Bảo tàng góp phần bảo vệ di sản văn hóa của khu vực.
audio_word:
audio_example:
accepted_answers: heritage
source: Oxford 5000 by CEFR level, tr. 5, dòng: heritage n. [C1]. Nguồn nhà xuất bản: https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/external/pdf/wordlists/oxford-3000-5000/The_Oxford_5000_by_CEFR_level.pdf ; Bản lưu dùng đối chiếu: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ittuann/The-Oxford-5000-Word-Lists/7dc22b8ff8e5cb3410e962b0511d22cdf74b120c/The_Oxford_5000_by_CEFR_level.pdf
